# G-family aggregate — ELAI deep feature discovery

## Document contract

- **Family:** G (trust READ / EVIDENCE / RUNTIME / MEASUREMENT side; G-0..G-10).
- **Inputs:** `DEEP-G-001`, `DEEP-G-002`, and `07-G-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md`.
- **Policy:** Every distinct atomic record is preserved with its ID and source anchor (ledger file + line). No prose compression. No silent drop of minor atoms.
- **Namespacing:** Deep atoms keep native IDs under ledger prefix `G-001/` or `G-002/`. Summary atoms use `SUMMARY/`. Cross-file IX/INF/SD/A/C stay namespaced by ledger (e.g. `G-001/DG001-XINT-003`, `G-002/DG002-SD-014`).
- **NEWER-PLAN PRECEDENCE (within family):** An explicit SUPERSEDED/replacement/CORRECT marker in a source beats everything; otherwise a higher plan number is newer and wins the **target state** description. Losing descriptions are retained as superseded notes with anchors. Deep ledgers supersede the summary orientation pass where they conflict.
- **Status language preserved:** PLANNED / SHIPPED / WIRE-DARK / DEFERRED / ABSENT / runpod_pending are not collapsed.

## Notation

- `[E]` explicit · `[I]` inference · `[A]` ambiguity · `[C]` conflict/correction · `[Δ]` summary delta · `[status]` · `[superseded]` · `[SURFACE]` user-facing surface.
- Evidence subclasses from G-002: `E-P` planned/normative, `E-S` shipped-state assertion, `E-D` deferred, `E-G` gate, `E-R` risk/fallback.
- Anchors: `G0`/`G1`/…/`G10` = G-plan originals; `GSI` = G-SUMMARY_INDEX.md; `SUM` = summary discovery orientation.

## Per-unit atomic records

Feature and unit-local records grouped by G-N / ledger. Cross-file interactions, summary deltas, and ambiguity/conflict registers follow in dedicated sections.

- **G-001/DG001-G1-E001** [E] area=`Status/dependency` anchor=`G1:L1-L3`: Status is `PLANNED`, Wave 1, in-process and fail-closed; G-1 unlocks G-3 and G-7. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G1-E001)_

- **G-001/DG001-G1-E003** [E] area=`Core workflow/security` anchor=`G1:L9-L16, L31-L36`: Label every ingest byte, propagate only causally relevant provenance, and deterministically gate an Untrusted context before a destructive sink. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G1-E003)_
- **G-001/DG001-G1-E004** [E] area=`Boundary/dependency` anchor=`G1:L12-L14, L18-L21, L56-L62`: G-1 is data-plane defense across tool-capable agents; F-3 remains the control-plane minimal-tool-set commitment using the Researcher role envelope. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G1-E004)_
- **G-001/DG001-G1-E005** [E] area=`Integration/data flow` anchor=`G1:L18-L21, L59-L60`: E-37 `classify_injection`/`ClassifierVerdict` seeds an Untrusted label on a malicious span. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G1-E005)_
- **G-001/DG001-G1-E006** [E] area=`Constraint/tiering` anchor=`G1:L15-L16`: Gate-time work is wholly W-10 Tier 1: no network, subprocess, or RunPod; estimated two sessions/eight-to-twelve hours. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G1-E006)_
- **G-001/DG001-G1-E007** [E] area=`Concurrency` anchor=`G1:L22-L24`: G-1 is parallel-safe with G-4 and G-2, but not concurrent with edits to safety provenance/capability modules or the live dispatch fan-in. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G1-E007)_
- **G-001/DG001-G1-E008** [E] area=`Security model` anchor=`G1:L38-L46`: The intended information-flow label is a four-point integrity × confidentiality lattice, with P-T blocking Untrusted integrity and P-F restricting confidentiality egress. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G1-E008)_

- **G-001/DG001-G3-E009** [E] area=`Concurrency` anchor=`G3:L25-L27`: Work is parallel-safe with G-2/G-4 but not with concurrent edits to the PreToolUse dispatcher, sandbox networking modules, or SSB write path. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G3-E009)_

- **G-001/DG001-G7-E005** [E] area=`Security predicate` anchor=`G7:L13-L15`: A context with Trusted secret, Untrusted content, and destructive/egress capability is the lethal trifecta. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G7-E005)_
- **G-001/DG001-G7-E006** [E] area=`Security/capability isolation` anchor=`G7:L13-L18`: Authored design routes only the Untrusted span through a type-constrained quarantined sub-LLM with no tool grants. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G7-E006)_
- **G-001/DG001-G7-E007** [E] area=`Calibration/claim boundary` anchor=`G7:L16-L18, L135-L139`: Platt rescaling is a post-hoc, model-size-agnostic heuristic; it is not a proof of calibrated guarantees for a 7B target. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G7-E007)_
- **G-001/DG001-G7-E008** [E] area=`Tiering/constraint` anchor=`G7:L17-L18`: Abstention boundary is in-process/fail-closed; authored quarantine call is local inference with no RunPod at gate time; estimated ten-to-fourteen hours. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G7-E008)_
- **G-001/DG001-G7-E009** [E] area=`Dependencies` anchor=`G7:L20-L24`: Required shipped dependencies are D-16 conformal surfaces, G-1 provenance/capability surfaces, provider `GGSignals`, F-4 `stdout_hash`, and the FIDES interface document. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G7-E009)_
- **G-001/DG001-G7-E010** [E] area=`Concurrency` anchor=`G7:L25-L26`: Parallel-safe with G-2/G-4/G-8, not with G-1 or concurrent edits to conformal calibration/SSB write path. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G7-E010)_
- **G-001/DG001-G7-E011** [E] area=`Deterministic algorithm/failure` anchor=`G7:L31-L35`: `decide(claim_score,tau)` fail-closes NaN to Abstain, clamps score to `[0,1]`, computes nonconformity `1-p`, and Abstains when nonconformity exceeds tau. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G7-E011)_

- **G-001/DG001-G7-E024** [E] area=`Runtime workflow` anchor=`G7:L112-L116`: At live per-change evaluation, score feeds unchanged `decide`; Abstain holds the change and emits confidence evidence. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G7-E024)_

- **G-001/DG001-G7-C001** [C] area=`Corrected integration` anchor=`G7:L176-L183`: Dated correction says the live producer replaces a fake score in `task_runner.rs::dispatch_conformal_refusal_per_claim_hook`; `passing_stdout_hashes(test_rows)` is the nonconstant F-4 input and unchanged conformal `decide` remains the consumer. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G7-C001)_
- **G-001/DG001-G7-C002** [C] area=`Signal/status correction` anchor=`G7:L184-L185`: Dated DD1 correction says local GG log-prob threading is DEFERRED and cloud uses test plus self-consistency; this narrows the authored claim that all three signals feed every live score. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G7-C002)_
- **G-001/DG001-G7-E037** [E] area=`Dependencies/security` anchor=`G7:L186-L187`: Corrected quarantine consumes `is_egress_tool`, G-9-backed argv-scope `context_holds_secret`, and G-1 `tainted_context`. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G7-E037)_
- **G-001/DG001-G7-E038** [E] area=`End-to-end workflow` anchor=`G7:L188-L192`: Corrected confidence flow is live per-change hook → score using real test signal → unchanged conformal decision → held change on Abstain → `emit_claim_confidence_scored`. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G7-E038)_
- **G-001/DG001-G7-C003** [C] area=`Corrected security behavior` anchor=`G7:L193-L200`: Corrected production trifecta flow runs before `git_commit`, detects secret+Untrusted+egress, emits `trifecta_quarantined { quarantined=false, sub_llm_verdict="not_run_blocked" }`, and returns `Err` for a hard block. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G7-C003)_
- **G-001/DG001-G7-C004** [C] area=`Deferred correction` anchor=`G7:L197-L200`: The no-tool sub-LLM primitive is built and tested but not driven in production because no live client reaches the free-function chokepoint; live routing is explicitly DEFERRED. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G7-C004)_
- **G-001/DG001-G7-E039** [E] area=`Security invariant/fallback` anchor=`G7:L197-L200`: The load-bearing production guarantee is that hard refusal prevents the Untrusted span reaching the real tool even though no sub-LLM ran. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G7-E039)_
- **G-001/DG001-G7-E040** [E] area=`Constraints/non-goals` anchor=`G7:L202-L210`: Stub bans prohibit fabricated/constant score, no-tool leakage, default-off ENABLE, overclaimed UniCR, BLAKE2/SHA2, paper-only code citation, and wrong UniCR ID. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G7-E040)_
- **G-001/DG001-G7-E041** [E] area=`Acceptance trigger` anchor=`G7:L214-L217`: Authored observable fixture combines low-consistency/failing-test change with secret+injected Untrusted+destructive-tool context. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G7-E041)_
- **G-001/DG001-G7-E042** [E] area=`Authored acceptance` anchor=`G7:L218-L222`: Authored expected evidence is low-score `abstained=true` and `trifecta_quarantined` with `quarantined=true`; artifact requires held change, no tool reach, clean FP control, and drawer rendering. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G7-E042)_
- **G-001/DG001-G7-C005** [C] area=`Acceptance conflict` anchor=`G7:L193-L200, L214-L222`: The authored expectation `quarantined=true` directly conflicts with corrected production evidence `quarantined=false`/`not_run_blocked`. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G7-C005)_
- **G-001/DG001-G7-C006** [C] area=`Tooling/test correction` anchor=`G7:L224-L243`: Corrected deterministic process removes nonexistent `--run-trigger` opt-in and places the combined Tier-1 contract under `crates/orchestrator/tests`, not `crates/contracts/tests`. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G7-C006)_
- **G-001/DG001-G7-E043** [E] area=`Verification` anchor=`G7:L224-L243`: Deterministic checks grep all score/quarantine APIs, live task/sink wires, both event cascades/goldens, UI/test/decision doc, forbid ENABLE/wrong UniCR/BLAKE2/SHA2, and run cross-language diff. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G7-E043)_
- **G-001/DG001-G7-C007** [C] area=`Stale done-gate conflict` anchor=`G7:L245-L248, L264-L286`: Acceptance and W-1 text still require `--run-trigger PASS` and “the untrusted span was quarantined,” despite corrected tooling and production block-only semantics. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G7-C007)_
- **G-001/DG001-G7-E044** [E] area=`Done gate` anchor=`G7:L245-L262`: Remaining acceptance requires default-on abstention, real production entrypoints, dashboard E2E, seeded byte-identical score recomputation, honest evidence labels, bidirectional FP guards, event round trips, schema 216/493, cargo/auditor green, and no new dependency. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G7-E044)_
- **G-001/DG001-G7-E045** [E] area=`Tests/invariant` anchor=`G7:L258`: FP guards require high-confidence clean changes not to abstain and Trusted-only/missing-leg flows not to trigger quarantine. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G7-E045)_
- **G-001/DG001-G7-E046** [E] area=`Observability/schema evidence` anchor=`G7:L278-L279`: W-9 final golden ordinals are 0069 for `claim_confidence_scored` and 0464 for `trifecta_quarantined`. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G7-E046)_
- **G-001/DG001-G7-E047** [E] area=`Failure/tiering` anchor=`G7:L280-L285`: Tier 1 is in-process signal fusion/decision plus hard trifecta block; missing signals lower confidence and trifecta returns `Err`. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G7-E047)_
- **G-001/DG001-G7-E048** [E] area=`Deferred/tiering` anchor=`G7:L280-L286`: A real agent run is separately Tier 2/RunPod-gated; production live sub-LLM routing remains a documented follow-up. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G7-E048)_
- **G-001/DG001-G7-E049** [E] area=`Tests` anchor=`G7:L291-L300`: Combined contract covers low score→Abstain/event, high score→Answer, missing-signal degradation, seeded recompute, monotone Platt, score sensitivity to F-4, captured no-tool quarantine, missing-leg FP guard, and both event round trips. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G7-E049)_
- **G-001/DG001-G7-E050** [E] area=`Change surfaces` anchor=`G7:L302-L318`: Corrected change surfaces create score/quarantine/SSB-adapter/UI/test/decision files and modify orchestrator lib, task runner, production RPC, SSB/locks, and dashboard registration. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G7-E050)_
- **G-001/DG001-G7-C008** [C] area=`Schema conflict` anchor=`G7:L126-L133, L193-L200`: Event schema declares no `sub_llm_verdict`, but corrected production emission explicitly names that field; the source does not state a corresponding schema correction. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G7-C008)_
- **G-001/DG001-G7-A001** [A] area=`Data-flow ambiguity` anchor=`G7:L176-L185, L340-L348`: Corrected live confidence wire explicitly names real test hashes but does not pin how local deferred log-probs or k-sample self-consistency values are populated at that call; exact per-lane feature vector remains unresolved. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G7-A001)_

- **G-001/DG001-G7-A003** [A] area=`Predicate ambiguity` anchor=`G7:L13-L15, L52-L55, L118-L124, L186-L200`: The source alternates between “destructive tool,” “destructive/egress,” and corrected `is_egress_tool`; exact sink universe and whether non-egress destructive tools satisfy the third leg are not reconciled. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G7-A003)_
- **G-001/DG001-G7-C009** [C] area=`Dependency conflict` anchor=`G7:L3, L20-L26, L186-L200`: The corrected `context_holds_secret` explicitly bridges to G-9, adding a dependency absent from the header/pre-requisite/parallel-safety declarations. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G7-C009)_
- **G-001/DG001-G7-A004** [A] area=`Lifecycle ambiguity` anchor=`G7:L340-L346`: Cold-start Platt behavior is only “identity-ish” with low-confidence-by-default; calibration-data selection, persistence, and transition out of cold start are not specified. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G7-A004)_
- **G-001/DG001-G7-A005** [A] area=`Config/cost ambiguity` anchor=`G7:L347-L348`: k-sample self-consistency cost is unresolved; the decision doc must choose between k sampling and generation novelty alone. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G7-A005)_
- **G-001/DG001-G7-E051** [E] area=`Measurement/release risk` anchor=`G7:L340-L344`: Before trusting calibration, measure both over- and under-abstention behavior against D-17’s 5%–50% honest-refusal SLA; ELAI’s calibration error has no baseline. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G7-E051)_
- **G-001/DG001-G7-E052** [E] area=`Evidence/claim boundary` anchor=`G7:L349-L351`: UniCR remains unverified single-author evidence; only general conformal-risk-control grounding may support stronger theory claims. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G7-E052)_
- **G-001/DG001-G7-C010** [C] area=`Status conflict` anchor=`G7:L3, L66-L70, L88-L89, L126-L133, L291-L318`: Overall status remains `PLANNED`, while dated corrections call the producer, schemas, tests, and end-state “SHIPPED”; no explicit overall DONE transition is present. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G7-C010)_
- **G-001/DG001-G7-E053** [E] area=`Interactions` anchor=`G7:L353-L358`: Downstream, G-5 reads low-confidence first; G-2 records both events; G-1 supplies taint; F-3 plus containment forms control/data-plane defense; the producer also enables D-17 refusal measurement. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-001.discovery.md; native-id: DG001-G7-E053)_

- **G-002/DG002-G2-005** [E-P] anchor=`G2:L6, L81-L82, L114-L118, L268-L269`: Bundle integrity is a BLAKE3 Merkle root over stream, evidence manifest, and pins. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G2-005)_
- **G-002/DG002-G2-006** [E-P] anchor=`G2:L8-L9, L84-L90`: `elai replay --verify` replays the recorded action stream and rechecks all available F-gate verdict hashes, returning success or the first divergence. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G2-006)_
- **G-002/DG002-G2-007** [E-P] anchor=`G2:L10-L12, L38-L44`: The honest headline is reproducible action stream plus divergence verification, not unconditional bit-identical token replay. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G2-007)_
- **G-002/DG002-G2-008** [E-S] anchor=`G2:L15-L16, L35-L36, L46-L48`: The source asserts an append-only SQLite-WAL SSB with typed `write_payload`, action-type golden lock, and hashed tool I/O already ships. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G2-008)_
- **G-002/DG002-G2-009** [E-S] anchor=`G2:L16-L18, L49-L51, L64`: `ELAI_SEED`, `HardwareClass`, and `system_fingerprint` primitives are asserted shipped, but their canonical module path must be confirmed at authoring. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G2-009)_
- **G-002/DG002-G2-010** [E-S] anchor=`G2:L18-L20, L52-L53`: F-1/F-4/F-9 verdict fields are asserted shipped; F-10 is consumed when it lands rather than reimplemented. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G2-010)_

- **G-002/DG002-G2-012** [E-P] anchor=`G2:L22-L23`: G-2 may proceed in parallel with G-1 and G-4, and may ingest G-1 `taint_flow` rows. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G2-012)_

- **G-002/DG002-G2-021** [E-P] anchor=`G2:L79-L81`: `build_rra(&Ssb, seed, pins)` reads the session SSB rows and extracts the F-gate manifest. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G2-021)_
- **G-002/DG002-G2-022** [E-P] anchor=`G2:L58-L59, L81-L82`: `write_rra` serializes with `rmp-serde` plus a confirmed in-tree compression codec. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G2-022)_
- **G-002/DG002-G2-023** [E-P] anchor=`G2:L84-L88`: `ReplayVerdict` has `Verified`, `Divergence { first_event_id, expected_blake3, got_blake3 }`, and `Tampered`. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G2-023)_
- **G-002/DG002-G2-024** [E-P] anchor=`G2:L84-L85`: Verification first rereads the artifact and recomputes the Merkle root as the tamper check. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G2-024)_
- **G-002/DG002-G2-025** [E-P] anchor=`G2:L85-L86`: Verification freshly recomputes each gate verdict where its inputs are present. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G2-025)_
- **G-002/DG002-G2-026** [E-P] anchor=`G2:L86-L87`: Action replay runs under the pinned `ELAI_SEED` and reports the first mismatching event and hashes. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G2-026)_
- **G-002/DG002-G2-027** [E-R] anchor=`G2:L88-L90, L151-L154`: A cloud-opaque or body-GC'd step that cannot be reexecuted must never produce a fabricated `Verified`; it reports the lower guarantee explicitly. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G2-027)_
- **G-002/DG002-G2-028** [E-P] anchor=`G2:L141-L150, L176, L189-L191`: The reconciled production emission point is `run_subcommand.rs::run_async` exit via `emit_run_artifact`, mirroring `--export-ssb`, using a clone of the live `wiring.ssb` Arc. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G2-028)_
- **G-002/DG002-G2-029** [E-P] anchor=`G2:L92-L95, L146-L150`: A completed run writes `workflow/sessions/{id}/run.rra` and exactly one `run_artifact_emitted` row. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G2-029)_
- **G-002/DG002-G2-030** [E-P] anchor=`G2:L94-L95, L151-L155, L188`: RRA emission is default-on; the sole runtime switch is kill switch `ELAI_RRA_DISABLE`, and `ELAI_RRA_ENABLE` is forbidden. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G2-030)_
- **G-002/DG002-G2-031** [E-P] anchor=`G2:L97-L100`: CLI surfaces are `elai run --emit-rra` (force emit), `elai rra show <path>`, and `elai replay --verify <path>`, all supporting harness-convention `--json`. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G2-031)_
- **G-002/DG002-G2-032** [E-P] anchor=`G2:L97-L100, L166-L168`: `rra show` renders seed, pins, gate hashes, guarantee level, and root; replay prints `Verified`, `Divergence@event`, or `Tampered`. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G2-032)_
- **G-002/DG002-G2-033** [E-P] anchor=`G2:L102-L104`: `RunArtifactEmittedPayload` contains `root_blake3`, string `guarantee_level`, `gate_count`, `seed`, and `session_id`. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G2-033)_
- **G-002/DG002-G2-034** [E-P] anchor=`G2:L102-L104, L198`: `RunArtifactEmitted` requires SQL spelling/parsing/CHECK, payload round trip, golden/action-type lock, and a named baseline-plus-one schema bump. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G2-034)_
- **G-002/DG002-G2-035** [E-P] anchor=`G2:L106-L108, L160-L162`: `RraViewerPanel.svelte` plus `commands_g2.rs::get_run_artifact` renders manifest fields and the replay verdict and must pass cross-language drift checking. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G2-035)_
- **G-002/DG002-G2-036** [E-D] anchor=`G2:L110-L112, L217-L218, L270`: Resume from the RRA stream is an optional, separately gated follow-on that closes `recover_from_checkpoint`; it is not part of G-2 DONE. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G2-036)_
- **G-002/DG002-G2-037** [E-P] anchor=`G2:L114-L118`: `G-2-decisions.md` records guarantee levels, cloud/body capture limits, Merkle coverage, “reproduced not correct,” and the resume-vs-BSP correction. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G2-037)_
- **G-002/DG002-G2-038** [E-P] anchor=`G2:L120-L132`: Produced APIs are `RunArtifact`, `GuaranteeLevel`, `build_rra`, `write_rra`, `verify_rra`, `ReplayVerdict`, `RunArtifactEmitted`, `RunArtifactEmittedPayload`, CLI `rra_cmd::run`, `get_run_artifact`, and `RraViewerPanel`. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G2-038)_
- **G-002/DG002-G2-039** [E-P] anchor=`G2:L133-L150`: Consumed production surfaces are live `Ssb`, three named F payloads, `run_async`, determinism pins, and CLI dispatch. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G2-039)_
- **G-002/DG002-G2-040** [E-P] anchor=`G2:L70, L82, L155-L159`: Production code must use BLAKE3 only for roots/event hashes and contain no `.unwrap()`/`.expect()` on RRA paths. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G2-040)_
- **G-002/DG002-G2-041** [E-P] anchor=`G2:L58-L59, L157-L158, L200, L266-L267`: No dependency is assumed beyond in-tree `rmp-serde`, compression, and BLAKE3; zstd requires explicit verification and user confirmation. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G2-041)_
- **G-002/DG002-G2-042** [E-G] anchor=`G2:L163-L168`: The live observable runs a fixture with `run --emit-rra`, then verifies the generated session artifact. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G2-042)_
- **G-002/DG002-G2-043** [E-G] anchor=`G2:L164-L165`: That run must leave a live `run_artifact_emitted` row carrying root, guarantee level, and gate count. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G2-043)_
- **G-002/DG002-G2-044** [E-G] anchor=`G2:L166-L168`: The artifact must exist, show seed/pins/verdicts, return verified or first divergence as appropriate, and change to `Tampered` after a one-byte mutation. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G2-044)_
- **G-002/DG002-G2-045** [E-G] anchor=`G2:L170-L183, L185-L187`: DONE requires `wiring_contract_check.py --run-trigger` and a real artifact/event/verdict; cargo-green alone is insufficient. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G2-045)_
- **G-002/DG002-G2-046** [E-G] anchor=`G2:L188`: Default-on behavior must be proven with no override and suppression with `ELAI_RRA_DISABLE=1`. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G2-046)_
- **G-002/DG002-G2-047** [E-G] anchor=`G2:L189-L191, L202-L207`: Production-entry acceptance is the reconciled `run_async` exit path plus real CLI paths, not test-only and not the stale dispatcher anchor. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G2-047)_
- **G-002/DG002-G2-048** [E-G] anchor=`G2:L192-L193`: A local seeded fixture must verify, a cloud fixture must remain `VerifyDivergence`-checked, and a byte tamper must be `Tampered`. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G2-048)_
- **G-002/DG002-G2-049** [E-G] anchor=`G2:L194, L208-L210`: Dashboard-smoker must prove the manifest and verdict through `RraViewerPanel`. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G2-049)_
- **G-002/DG002-G2-050** [E-G] anchor=`G2:L195-L196, L219-L220`: Composition acceptance requires reading, not reimplementing, F-1/F-4/F-9 verdict fields for a run that exercised them. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G2-050)_
- **G-002/DG002-G2-051** [E-G] anchor=`G2:L197`: The root must recompute byte-identically from the SSB stream under the pinned seed. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G2-051)_
- **G-002/DG002-G2-052** [E-G] anchor=`G2:L198-L200`: Completion also requires payload round trip/golden lock, workspace cargo check, auditor passes for orchestrator/CLI/contracts, and no unverified dependency. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G2-052)_
- **G-002/DG002-G2-053** [E-G] anchor=`G2:L222-L225`: The round-trip contract builds from a seeded fixture, recomputes the root, round-trips the file, and confirms F-1/F-4/F-9 manifest entries. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G2-053)_
- **G-002/DG002-G2-054** [E-G] anchor=`G2:L226-L228`: Replay contract cases are local `Verified`, byte-tampered `Tampered`, cloud `VerifyDivergence`-checked, and injected event-N `Divergence`. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G2-054)_
- **G-002/DG002-G2-055** [E-G] anchor=`G2:L229-L230`: A smoke test must drive the real run-end hook and observe both `run.rra` and the event row. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G2-055)_
- **G-002/DG002-G2-056** [E-R] anchor=`G2:L260-L263`: Capture completeness is explicitly unresolved for response bodies retained outside hashed SSB rows and bounds re-execution claims. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G2-056)_
- **G-002/DG002-G2-057** [E-R] anchor=`G2:L268-L269`: The root must cover every stream/manifest/pin byte so any mutation is detectable. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G2-057)_
- **G-002/DG002-G2-058** [E-P] anchor=`G2:L272-L276`: Downstream composition is G-5 per-hunk evidence, G-6 replayable benchmarks, G-8 signed predicates, and G-9 export-safety precondition. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G2-058)_

### Unit `G-6` — from G-002 (69 records)

Source unit heading: G-6 — SAFEDELTA competitive safety-delta benchmark
Ledger: `DEEP-G-002.discovery.md`

- **G-002/DG002-G6-001** [E-D] anchor=`G6:L1-L3, L14-L16`: G-6 is a planned Wave-3, multi-session benchmark deferred behind F-2b LIVE and G-2; its live model/task arm is RunPod-gated. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G6-001)_
- **G-002/DG002-G6-002** [E-P] anchor=`G6:L5-L6, L31-L33`: SAFEDELTA’s primary design is same-model gate-ON versus gate-OFF ablation on git-stripped held-out tasks. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G6-002)_
- **G-002/DG002-G6-003** [E-P] anchor=`G6:L5-L6, L31-L32`: The target metric is the gate’s marginal residual-insecure rate that reaches a human reviewer. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G6-003)_
- **G-002/DG002-G6-004** [E-P] anchor=`G6:L6, L11-L16`: Competitor-harness head-to-head comparison is secondary to the ELAI ablation. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G6-004)_
- **G-002/DG002-G6-005** [E-S] anchor=`G6:L7-L10, L18-L21, L33-L38`: `SecCodePltRunner` is asserted to already compute `insecure_rate_before`, residual `insecure_rate_after`, and `gate_recall` over an embedded CWE corpus. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G6-005)_
- **G-002/DG002-G6-006** [E-S] anchor=`G6:L9-L11, L20-L21, L69-L74`: The existing `InsecureRateMeasured` static-gate beacon fires only inside a real `elai bench seccodeplt` invocation and is explicitly asserted never to have run live. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G6-006)_
- **G-002/DG002-G6-007** [E-P] anchor=`G6:L11-L13, L41-L46`: G-6 generalizes that static fixed-corpus ablation into a live model/task, dynamic-exploit, anti-gaming, multi-suite matrix. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G6-007)_
- **G-002/DG002-G6-008** [E-P] anchor=`G6:L12-L14, L46-L48`: The report headline is “fewer dynamically exploitable diffs per 100 tasks reach a reviewer,” with 95% bootstrap CI and per-CWE breakdown. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G6-008)_
- **G-002/DG002-G6-009** [E-P] anchor=`G6:L13-L16, L47-L48, L164-L170, L207-L208`: SAFEDELTA must never be framed as a SWE-bench/model-capability rank, a “beats/#1” claim, or a perfect-code promise. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G6-009)_
- **G-002/DG002-G6-010** [E-P] anchor=`G6:L18-L24, L69-L72, L237-L238`: G-6 consumes F-2b’s static/dynamic floor and does not redefine F-2. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G6-010)_
- **G-002/DG002-G6-011** [E-P] anchor=`G6:L23-L24, L81, L123-L127`: Each benchmark run is wrapped by G-2’s RRA so the delta and signed manifest are independently replayable. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G6-011)_
- **G-002/DG002-G6-012** [E-P] anchor=`G6:L25-L26`: G-6 may proceed in parallel with G-3/G-4 but not concurrently with edits to `seccodeplt_runner.rs` or bench CLI dispatch. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G6-012)_
- **G-002/DG002-G6-013** [E-S] anchor=`G6:L50-L58, L75-L77`: The real existing bench route is `args_bench.rs` → `bench_cmd.rs` → per-suite files; `crates/cli/src/benchmark.rs` does not exist. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G6-013)_
- **G-002/DG002-G6-014** [E-S] anchor=`G6:L53-L64, L75-L77`: `parse_bench_seccodeplt_args` accepts only `--json`, not `--suite`; only `run_tier_eval` supplies an existing `--suites` pattern. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G6-014)_
- **G-002/DG002-G6-015** [E-S] anchor=`G6:L58-L64, L73-L80`: `InsecureRateMeasured` is a FLAT beacon, while `run_bench_ablation` supplies an existing gate-on/off dashboard precedent. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G6-015)_
- **G-002/DG002-G6-016** [E-P] anchor=`G6:L22, L43, L61-L62, L78`: Matrix comparisons pin `ELAI_SEED` and `HardwareClass` and are valid only within the same hardware class. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G6-016)_

- **G-002/DG002-G6-030** [E-P] anchor=`G6:L100-L102`: `SafeDeltaReport` carries per-suite and per-CWE cells, delta/CI, and before/after dynamically exploitable counts. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G6-030)_
- **G-002/DG002-G6-031** [E-P] anchor=`G6:L100-L103`: `safety_delta` is `(before - after)` exploitable diffs per 100 tasks and is paired with a 95% bootstrap interval. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G6-031)_

- **G-002/DG002-G6-033** [E-P] anchor=`G6:L105-L106`: Task checkouts remove `.git` so models cannot inspect git history or the known fix. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G6-033)_

- **G-002/DG002-G6-036** [E-P] anchor=`G6:L107-L109, L164-L167`: A dynamic-suite diff counts insecure only when its exploit actually fires in the sandbox; static-pattern-only scoring is forbidden. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G6-036)_

- **G-002/DG002-G8-063** [E-D] anchor=`G8:L298-L299`: Offline local Merkle carries DONE; cosign-keyless is the separately gated future path. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G8-063)_
- **G-002/DG002-G8-064** [E-P] anchor=`G8:L304-L308`: G-8 joins G-2 replayability and G-6 measurement into the signed, offline-verifiable trust spine; G-5 may display status. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G8-064)_

### Unit `G-9` — from G-002 (65 records)

Source unit heading: G-9 — write-time journal/log redaction and retention
Ledger: `DEEP-G-002.discovery.md`

- **G-002/DG002-G9-001** [E-P] anchor=`G9:L1-L3, L12-L15`: G-9 is a planned Wave-2, local-only, no-new-dependency redaction/retention feature and an export-safety precondition for G-2/G-8. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-001)_
- **G-002/DG002-G9-002** [E-P] anchor=`G9:L5-L7, L17-L21`: Redaction runs at both the sole SSB writer and the hook-log sink. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-002)_
- **G-002/DG002-G9-003** [E-P] anchor=`G9:L5-L10, L30-L37`: Every detected secret/PII span is transformed before persistence; post-write scrubbing is not the design. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-003)_
- **G-002/DG002-G9-004** [E-P] anchor=`G9:L6-L7, L35-L37`: Replacements are stable tokens so repeated occurrences correlate without revealing the original value. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-004)_
- **G-002/DG002-G9-005** [E-P] anchor=`G9:L7-L10, L32-L33`: The append-only `no UPDATE/DELETE` SSB invariant forces write-time mutation before INSERT. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-005)_
- **G-002/DG002-G9-006** [E-R] anchor=`G9:L34-L36, L95-L101`: A detector error fails closed by redacting the field instead of passing the raw value. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-006)_
- **G-002/DG002-G9-007** [E-P] anchor=`G9:L10-L12, L40-L44, L74-L75`: Detection reuses F-1 `vibegate_secrets.rs::SECRET_PATTERNS`/`SECRET_LABELS`; G-9 must not define a second regex set. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-007)_
- **G-002/DG002-G9-008** [E-P] anchor=`G9:L12-L15, L40-L44, L63-L64`: Presidio, ggshield, and cloud redaction services are forbidden dependencies/egress paths; the implementation is hand-rolled and local. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-008)_
- **G-002/DG002-G9-009** [E-R] anchor=`G9:L13-L15, L43-L44`: Regex/pattern detection is explicitly treated as high-recall/low-precision and its over-redaction rate is a release gate. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-009)_
- **G-002/DG002-G9-010** [E-S] anchor=`G9:L17-L20, L46-L50`: The asserted SSB chain is `write_payload` → `Ssb::write` → sole `write_loop` → `execute_write`, with payload bound between `BEGIN IMMEDIATE` and `COMMIT`. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-010)_
- **G-002/DG002-G9-011** [E-S] anchor=`G9:L20-L21, L52-L54`: The asserted hook sink is `log_skill_invocation`, appending JSON lines to per-session `hook-executions.log`; its read side is `get_hook_executions`. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-011)_
- **G-002/DG002-G9-012** [E-S] anchor=`G9:L55-L58, L74-L75`: Existing secret shapes include AWS keys, typed/generic PEM headers, and secret assignments; CWE mapping detail beyond those surfaces is verify-before-cite. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-012)_
- **G-002/DG002-G9-013** [E-S] anchor=`G9:L58-L60, L76`: Stable-token precedents are `source_blake3_prefix` and `blake3::hash(label)`, with workspace BLAKE3 already present. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-013)_
- **G-002/DG002-G9-014** [E-S] anchor=`G9:L60-L63, L77-L78`: The database is one shared `<base>/.elai_cc/sessions/ssb.db` with `session_id` as a column, not one database per session. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-014)_
- **G-002/DG002-G9-015** [E-S] anchor=`G9:L60-L63, L77-L78`: Only `hook-executions.log` is a per-session, file-granularity sink. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-015)_
- **G-002/DG002-G9-016** [E-S] anchor=`G9:L50-L52, L70-L71`: `run_migration` has a destructive schema-mismatch DROP, distinct from retention and ordinary append-only operation. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-016)_
- **G-002/DG002-G9-017** [E-S] anchor=`G9:L62-L64, L79-L80`: An optional wire-dark `secret_redacted` advisory follows existing env-gated event precedent; it is not required for the redaction itself. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-017)_
- **G-002/DG002-G9-018** [E-P] anchor=`G9:L24-L25`: G-9 may proceed alongside G-3/G-4/G-8 but must coordinate with G-2 and any edits to `execute_write`. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-018)_
- **G-002/DG002-G9-019** [E-P] anchor=`G9:L93-L98`: `redact(text) -> (String, u32)` returns transformed text plus redaction count. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-019)_
- **G-002/DG002-G9-020** [E-P] anchor=`G9:L95-L99`: Replacement syntax is `[REDACTED:<label>:<blake3-16>]`, derived from the secret value. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-020)_
- **G-002/DG002-G9-021** [E-P] anchor=`G9:L97-L99`: Identical secret values must map to identical replacement tokens. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-021)_
- **G-002/DG002-G9-022** [E-P] anchor=`G9:L99-L101, L299-L300`: `redact_payload(&mut SsbPayload)` walks and mutates all text-bearing payload fields in place. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-022)_
- **G-002/DG002-G9-023** [E-P] anchor=`G9:L99-L101`: Redaction production paths contain no `.unwrap()`/`.expect()`. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-023)_
- **G-002/DG002-G9-024** [E-P] anchor=`G9:L103-L106`: `execute_write` invokes `redact_payload` at its top, before payload binding/INSERT, so raw values never land in `ssb.db`. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-024)_
- **G-002/DG002-G9-025** [E-P] anchor=`G9:L105-L106, L126-L129`: Core redaction is default-on with the only kill switch `ELAI_REDACT_DISABLE`; `ELAI_REDACT_ENABLE` is forbidden. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-025)_
- **G-002/DG002-G9-026** [E-P] anchor=`G9:L108-L109`: The serialized hook `LogLine` is passed through `redact()` before append. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-026)_
- **G-002/DG002-G9-027** [E-P] anchor=`G9:L111-L112`: `purge_expired(base, ttl) -> PurgeReport` is the retention API. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-027)_
- **G-002/DG002-G9-028** [E-P] anchor=`G9:L111-L114`: An expired per-session hook log is deleted as a whole file. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-028)_
- **G-002/DG002-G9-029** [E-P] anchor=`G9:L113-L117, L292-L295`: Shared-SSB retention must choose and document either session-scoped maintenance-boundary row purge or whole-file TTL across all sessions. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-029)_
- **G-002/DG002-G9-030** [E-P] anchor=`G9:L115-L117, L166`: Mid-session SSB UPDATE/DELETE is forbidden even for TTL. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-030)_
- **G-002/DG002-G9-031** [E-D] anchor=`G9:L119-L121, L143-L150`: `SecretRedacted`/payload is optional and, if emitted, contains only count, sink, and session ID—never the secret. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-031)_
- **G-002/DG002-G9-032** [E-D] anchor=`G9:L119-L124, L168-L169`: Only the optional advisory may be wire-dark behind `ELAI_REDACT_ADVISORY_ENABLE`; core redaction cannot be env-enable-gated. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-032)_
- **G-002/DG002-G9-033** [E-P] anchor=`G9:L121-L124`: If the advisory ships on the stated baseline, it gets SQL/parse/CHECK/golden and schema 205→206/variants 482→483. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-033)_
- **G-002/DG002-G9-034** [E-G] anchor=`G9:L126-L129`: Over-redaction is measured across at least three real orchestrate runs before default-on rollout. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-034)_
- **G-002/DG002-G9-035** [E-G] anchor=`G9:L127-L129`: A ten-benign-payload corpus must show zero false redactions and byte-preserve benign payloads. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-035)_
- **G-002/DG002-G9-036** [E-P] anchor=`G9:L131-L133`: `RedactionPanel` plus `commands_g9.rs::get_redaction_stats` shows per-session counts/advisories but never secret values. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-036)_
- **G-002/DG002-G9-037** [E-P] anchor=`G9:L135-L141`: `G-9-decisions.md` records write-time necessity, detector reuse/no deps, stable tokens, honest shared-DB retention, citation/penalty corrections, FP rollout, and G-2/G-8 precondition status. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-037)_
- **G-002/DG002-G9-038** [E-P] anchor=`G9:L143-L150`: Produced identifiers are `redact`, `redact_payload`, `purge_expired`, the stats getter/panel, and optional `SecretRedacted` event/payload. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-038)_
- **G-002/DG002-G9-039** [E-P] anchor=`G9:L151-L158`: Consumed surfaces are `execute_write`, `write_payload`, F-1 patterns/labels, hash-prefix precedent, hook writer, DB-layout constants, and G-2 export path. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-039)_
- **G-002/DG002-G9-040** [E-P] anchor=`G9:L159-L162`: Production reachability is SSB writer → payload redaction → INSERT and hook writer → line redaction → append. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-040)_
- **G-002/DG002-G9-041** [E-P] anchor=`G9:L163-L165`: No redaction data may leave the machine because a redactor that phones home is itself considered a leak. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-041)_
- **G-002/DG002-G9-042** [E-P] anchor=`G9:L164-L165`: No new detector/regex set and no Presidio/ggshield dependency may be introduced. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-042)_
- **G-002/DG002-G9-043** [E-P] anchor=`G9:L167`: A detection failure cannot silently pass through raw content. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-043)_
- **G-002/DG002-G9-044** [E-P] anchor=`G9:L170`: The implementation and documentation may not claim `ssb.db` is per-session. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-044)_
- **G-002/DG002-G9-045** [E-P] anchor=`G9:L171, L195`: BLAKE3 is the only permitted redaction-token digest; BLAKE2/SHA2 are forbidden. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-045)_
- **G-002/DG002-G9-046** [E-P] anchor=`G9:L172, L268-L275`: Refuted Presidio numerical claims must be omitted in favor of qualitative high-recall/low-precision wording. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-046)_
- **G-002/DG002-G9-047** [E-G] anchor=`G9:L177-L179`: The observable fixture carries an AWS key and email through prompt/tool output, triggers hook logging, and runs with no override. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-047)_
- **G-002/DG002-G9-048** [E-G] anchor=`G9:L180-L181`: The persisted row must contain `[REDACTED:aws_key:<blake3-16>]`, and searching the DB must find no raw `AKIA` key. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-048)_
- **G-002/DG002-G9-049** [E-G] anchor=`G9:L182-L184`: The hook log must contain only tokens, expired hook logs must purge whole, the panel must show counts, and benign data must remain byte-identical. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-049)_
- **G-002/DG002-G9-050** [E-G] anchor=`G9:L186-L203`: DONE requires wiring-checker success plus real redaction at both sinks and an expired-log purge; cargo-green alone is insufficient. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-050)_
- **G-002/DG002-G9-051** [E-G] anchor=`G9:L204-L205`: Default-on acceptance proves no-override redaction and kill-switch suppression without any core ENABLE flag. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-051)_
- **G-002/DG002-G9-052** [E-G] anchor=`G9:L206-L207`: Production acceptance requires both `execute_write` and `log_skill_invocation`, not test-only paths. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-052)_
- **G-002/DG002-G9-053** [E-G] anchor=`G9:L208`: Dashboard-smoker verifies count-only data through `RedactionPanel`. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-053)_
- **G-002/DG002-G9-054** [E-G] anchor=`G9:L209-L210`: A database-after-commit proof must show the raw secret never landed, because later scrubbing is prohibited. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-054)_
- **G-002/DG002-G9-055** [E-G] anchor=`G9:L211-L212`: Stable-token tests require identical values to map identically and benign payloads to remain unchanged. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-055)_
- **G-002/DG002-G9-056** [E-G] anchor=`G9:L213-L214`: The FP gate requires zero errors on ten benign samples and a measured three-real-run rate. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-056)_
- **G-002/DG002-G9-057** [E-G] anchor=`G9:L215-L216`: TTL acceptance deletes an expired hook log whole and proves no live-row UPDATE/DELETE; shared-DB retention is labelled session-scoped/whole-file. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-057)_
- **G-002/DG002-G9-058** [E-G] anchor=`G9:L217-L218`: If shipped, the advisory must round-trip/golden-lock and perform the conditional 205→206/482→483 bump. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-058)_
- **G-002/DG002-G9-059** [E-G] anchor=`G9:L219-L220`: Completion includes workspace cargo check/auditor and zero new dependency. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-059)_
- **G-002/DG002-G9-060** [E-P] anchor=`G9:L239-L243`: The complete feature is Tier-1 in-process and genuinely fail-closed, with no network/subprocess/RunPod work. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-060)_
- **G-002/DG002-G9-061** [E-G] anchor=`G9:L245-L249`: Write-time tests cover AWS key plus email, DB raw-value absence, repeated-token stability, benign identity, and detector-error fail-closed behavior. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-061)_
- **G-002/DG002-G9-062** [E-G] anchor=`G9:L250-L252`: TTL contracts cover whole-hook-log purge, no mid-session row mutation, and the chosen shared-DB retention mode. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-062)_
- **G-002/DG002-G9-063** [E-R] anchor=`G9:L287-L291`: The primary failure mode is corrupting real benign payloads by over-redaction; anchored credential-shape patterns plus measured FP gates are the mitigation. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-063)_
- **G-002/DG002-G9-064** [E-R] anchor=`G9:L299-L300`: Missing any text-bearing field in any payload variant is explicitly a leak hole and requires cross-variant contract coverage. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-064)_
- **G-002/DG002-G9-065** [E-P] anchor=`G9:L302-L306`: Once present, G-9 makes RRA/attestation export safer and establishes F-1’s detector as the single detection source for block and persistence-redaction decisions. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G9-065)_

### Unit `G-10` — from G-002 (77 records)

Source unit heading: G-10 — model-drift regression canary
Ledger: `DEEP-G-002.discovery.md`

- **G-002/DG002-G10-001** [E-P] anchor=`G10:L1-L3, L19-L26`: G-10 is a planned Wave-3 post-G-9 extension added 2026-06-02, not an R1–R9 roadmap initiative. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G10-001)_
- **G-002/DG002-G10-002** [E-P] anchor=`G10:L5-L9, L42-L52`: It monitors exogenous provider checkpoint/local-weight changes, a seam not covered by endogenous ELAI regressions. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G10-002)_
- **G-002/DG002-G10-003** [E-P] anchor=`G10:L9-L10, L54-L58`: Model identity is the `(model, fingerprint, lane)` tuple. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G10-003)_
- **G-002/DG002-G10-004** [E-P] anchor=`G10:L9-L11`: A small fixed canary set has baseline scores keyed to model identity. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G10-004)_
- **G-002/DG002-G10-005** [E-P] anchor=`G10:L10-L11`: Identity change or a schedule triggers a canary rerun. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G10-005)_
- **G-002/DG002-G10-006** [E-P] anchor=`G10:L11-L12`: Evaluation compares each canary family with the last-good baseline and computes a regression delta. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G10-006)_
- **G-002/DG002-G10-007** [E-P] anchor=`G10:L12-L13`: Regression emits `model_drift_suspected` and routes matching `ReasoningTemplate` IDs to review. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G10-007)_
- **G-002/DG002-G10-008** [E-P] anchor=`G10:L13, L64-L67`: Detection and surfacing are the default product boundary; model auto-swap/revert is excluded. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G10-008)_
- **G-002/DG002-G10-009** [E-P] anchor=`G10:L13-L16, L60-L64`: Local identities based on weights/version can be labelled fingerprint-confirmed when the canary also regresses. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G10-009)_
- **G-002/DG002-G10-010** [E-P] anchor=`G10:L14-L16, L60-L64`: Cloud identities with opaque `system_fingerprint` can only be labelled behavioral-drift-suspected. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G10-010)_
- **G-002/DG002-G10-011** [E-P] anchor=`G10:L64-L67`: A local false alarm must not automatically swap models because false remediation is considered worse than advisory drift. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G10-011)_
- **G-002/DG002-G10-012** [E-S] anchor=`G10:L19-L26, L42-L48`: Gap analysis asserts no model-version pin type exists and all existing canary/regression symbols key on endogenous changes. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G10-012)_
- **G-002/DG002-G10-013** [E-S] anchor=`G10:L23-L25, L50-L52, L75-L77`: `ModelSwap` and `TierModelSwap` exist but are wire-dark with no production emitter. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G10-013)_
- **G-002/DG002-G10-014** [E-S] anchor=`G10:L24-L26, L49-L50, L78-L79`: `autonomous_gate::substitute_model` is restart/VRAM-triggered, not regression-triggered. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G10-014)_
- **G-002/DG002-G10-015** [E-P] anchor=`G10:L19-L23`: External practitioner discourse is corroborative motivation only and not a requirement. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G10-015)_
- **G-002/DG002-G10-016** [E-P] anchor=`G10:L28-L29, L73-L74`: G-2 `RunArtifact` identity pins are a hard dependency or co-ship input. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G10-016)_
- **G-002/DG002-G10-017** [E-P] anchor=`G10:L29-L31, L69-L72`: D-7 `canary.rs` supplies `CanaryFamily`, `FamilyEvalResult`, delta, sample-count threshold, and non-determinism band and must be reused. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G10-017)_
- **G-002/DG002-G10-018** [E-P] anchor=`G10:L31, L84`: Existing `held_out::TaskId` fixtures supply the fixed canary tasks. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G10-018)_
- **G-002/DG002-G10-019** [E-P] anchor=`G10:L31-L33, L82-L83`: F-4 `TestOutcomePayload.stdout_hash`/deterministic pass rate supplies the malformed-output-spike signal. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G10-019)_
- **G-002/DG002-G10-020** [E-P] anchor=`G10:L33-L34, L80-L81`: D-1’s `ReasoningTemplate` archive/domain is the review/revert target. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G10-020)_
- **G-002/DG002-G10-021** [E-P] anchor=`G10:L35-L36`: G-10 may proceed with G-5/G-8/G-9, but not with G-2 or concurrent `canary.rs` edits. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G10-021)_
- **G-002/DG002-G10-022** [E-S] anchor=`G10:L42-L48`: Existing endogenous examples are F-10 security monotonicity, self-mod/staged eval, D-7 optimized-template transfer, and goal-Jaccard drift. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G10-022)_
- **G-002/DG002-G10-023** [E-P] anchor=`G10:L54-L58`: G-10 is composition: G-2 provides identity input, D-7 measurement, D-1 review target, and F-4 malformed-output evidence. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G10-023)_
- **G-002/DG002-G10-024** [E-P] anchor=`G10:L102-L104`: `ModelIdentity` fields are `model_pin`, optional `system_fingerprint`, `provider`, and `lane`. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G10-024)_
- **G-002/DG002-G10-025** [E-P] anchor=`G10:L103-L104`: `Lane` states are `Local` and `Cloud`. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G10-025)_
- **G-002/DG002-G10-026** [E-P] anchor=`G10:L103-L105`: `identity_key()` is BLAKE3 over the complete model/fingerprint/provider/lane tuple. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G10-026)_
- **G-002/DG002-G10-027** [E-P] anchor=`G10:L105-L106`: `DriftBaseline` stores identity key, per-family scores, capture time, and per-output BLAKE3 hashes. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G10-027)_
- **G-002/DG002-G10-028** [E-P] anchor=`G10:L106-L107`: `DriftSignal` variants are `FingerprintChanged`, `CanaryRegressed`, and `MalformedOutputSpike`. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G10-028)_
- **G-002/DG002-G10-029** [E-P] anchor=`G10:L107-L109`: `DriftVerdict` variants are `NoDrift`, `BehavioralDriftSuspected { signals, per_family_delta, affected_template_ids, confirmed_by_fingerprint }`, and `BaselineCaptured`. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G10-029)_
- **G-002/DG002-G10-030** [E-P] anchor=`G10:L111-L114`: `record_baseline` runs the fixed canary under the current model and `ELAI_SEED`, reuses family scoring, and persists scores/output hashes under the identity key. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G10-030)_
- **G-002/DG002-G10-031** [E-P] anchor=`G10:L111-L116`: `check_model_drift` compares current identity with baseline and records `FingerprintChanged`/confirmation only for the local lane. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G10-031)_
- **G-002/DG002-G10-032** [E-P] anchor=`G10:L116-L117`: The current canary reruns and uses D-7 sample-count/non-determinism logic; a delta within the band is not drift. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G10-032)_
- **G-002/DG002-G10-033** [E-P] anchor=`G10:L117-L118`: A seeded F-4 deterministic pass-rate window with malformed-output spike adds `MalformedOutputSpike`. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G10-033)_
- **G-002/DG002-G10-034** [E-P] anchor=`G10:L118-L120`: A beyond-band signal produces `BehavioralDriftSuspected`; affected templates are those whose domains match regressed families. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G10-034)_
- **G-002/DG002-G10-035** [E-P] anchor=`G10:L119-L120`: Cloud verdicts always set `confirmed_by_fingerprint=false`. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G10-035)_
- **G-002/DG002-G10-036** [E-R] anchor=`G10:L326-L327`: First observation of a model identity is a cold start returning `BaselineCaptured`, not drift. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G10-036)_
- **G-002/DG002-G10-037** [E-P] anchor=`G10:L122-L126`: At run completion, the runtime reads G-2 identity, compares provider/lane last-good state, calls the checker, and emits exactly one drift row on suspected drift. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G10-037)_
- **G-002/DG002-G10-038** [E-P] anchor=`G10:L126-L127`: Detection is default-on with only `ELAI_MODEL_DRIFT_DISABLE`; the auto-pin action remains off. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G10-038)_
- **G-002/DG002-G10-039** [E-P] anchor=`G10:L129-L133`: `elai model-drift baseline` captures or refreshes the current identity baseline. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G10-039)_
- **G-002/DG002-G10-040** [E-P] anchor=`G10:L129-L133`: `elai model-drift check` runs immediately and prints `NoDrift` or detailed suspected-drift output. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G10-040)_
- **G-002/DG002-G10-041** [E-P] anchor=`G10:L129-L133`: `elai model-drift show` displays stored baselines and the latest verdict. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G10-041)_
- **G-002/DG002-G10-042** [E-P] anchor=`G10:L133`: All model-drift CLI surfaces support harness-convention `--json`. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G10-042)_
- **G-002/DG002-G10-043** [E-P] anchor=`G10:L135-L140`: `ModelDriftSuspected` is described as one Pattern-2 action with `DriftSignal` as its discriminator. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G10-043)_
- **G-002/DG002-G10-044** [E-P] anchor=`G10:L135-L140`: `ModelDriftSuspectedPayload` contains `identity_key`, string signals, per-family deltas, affected-template IDs, fingerprint-confirmation flag, and baseline capture time. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G10-044)_
- **G-002/DG002-G10-045** [E-P] anchor=`G10:L139-L140`: The event requires SQL/parse/CHECK, payload round trip, golden/action lock, and a named baseline-plus-one schema bump. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G10-045)_
- **G-002/DG002-G10-046** [E-P] anchor=`G10:L142-L145`: `ModelDriftPanel.svelte` plus `get_model_drift_status` shows current identity, baseline/current bars, active signals, review queue, and honest lane badge. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G10-046)_
- **G-002/DG002-G10-047** [E-D] anchor=`G10:L147-L151`: Auto-pin-to-last-good is optional, outside DONE, default-off, human-gated, and only recommends feeding `substitute_model`. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G10-047)_
- **G-002/DG002-G10-048** [E-D] anchor=`G10:L147-L151`: Any auto-pin experiment requires wire-dark metadata `type=experiment`, owner, expiry, promotion-plan ID, and smoke test. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G10-048)_
- **G-002/DG002-G10-049** [E-G] anchor=`G10:L150-L151`: Auto-pin cannot enable until drift false positives are bounded on at least three real runs. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G10-049)_
- **G-002/DG002-G10-050** [E-P] anchor=`G10:L153-L157`: `G-10-decisions.md` records lane asymmetry, detection/action split, reused band, and compose-not-restate boundaries. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G10-050)_
- **G-002/DG002-G10-051** [E-P] anchor=`G10:L159-L171`: Produced identifiers are four model-drift types, baseline/check functions, event/payload, CLI command, status getter, and panel. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G10-051)_
- **G-002/DG002-G10-052** [E-P] anchor=`G10:L172-L181`: Consumed surfaces are D-7 family/result, held-out tasks, G-2 artifact, SSB/F-4 evidence, D-1 templates, runtime hook, and CLI dispatch. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G10-052)_
- **G-002/DG002-G10-053** [E-P] anchor=`G10:L182-L186`: Reachable CLI flows are baseline → `record_baseline` and check → verdict plus conditional event; production runtime and dashboard routes are also required. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G10-053)_
- **G-002/DG002-G10-054** [E-P] anchor=`G10:L187-L190`: Cloud drift may never fabricate `FingerprintConfirmed`; it remains behavioral with a false confirmation flag. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G10-054)_
- **G-002/DG002-G10-055** [E-P] anchor=`G10:L191-L193`: DONE contains no auto-swap/revert and no reimplementation of D-7 family/delta/band logic. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G10-055)_
- **G-002/DG002-G10-056** [E-P] anchor=`G10:L194-L195`: BLAKE3 is the only identity/output digest and production code contains no `.unwrap()`/`.expect()`. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G10-056)_
- **G-002/DG002-G10-057** [E-P] anchor=`G10:L196, L238`: No new dependency is allowed; canary, SSB, serialization, and BLAKE3 are all expected in-tree. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G10-057)_
- **G-002/DG002-G10-058** [E-G] anchor=`G10:L200-L203`: The observable first baselines fixture model A, then checks mock model B with a regressed canary output. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G10-058)_
- **G-002/DG002-G10-059** [E-G] anchor=`G10:L201-L203`: The drift row must include `CanaryRegressed`, local `FingerprintChanged`, family deltas, affected templates, and confirmation flag. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G10-059)_
- **G-002/DG002-G10-060** [E-G] anchor=`G10:L202-L203`: The CLI/panel must show suspected drift, while a clean model-A recheck inside the band returns `NoDrift` and emits no row. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G10-060)_
- **G-002/DG002-G10-061** [E-G] anchor=`G10:L203`: Per-family delta must recompute byte-identically from the SSB stream under pinned `ELAI_SEED`. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G10-061)_
- **G-002/DG002-G10-062** [E-G] anchor=`G10:L205-L222`: DONE requires the wiring checker, a real drift row, and a clean no-row FP guard; cargo-green alone is insufficient. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G10-062)_
- **G-002/DG002-G10-063** [E-G] anchor=`G10:L223-L224`: Default-on acceptance proves identity-change checking without override and suppression by `ELAI_MODEL_DRIFT_DISABLE=1`. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G10-063)_
- **G-002/DG002-G10-064** [E-G] anchor=`G10:L225-L226`: Production checking must fire through the named runtime path and the real model-drift CLI, not tests alone. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G10-064)_
- **G-002/DG002-G10-065** [E-G] anchor=`G10:L227-L229`: Local fixture acceptance requires both changed fingerprint and regressed canary with confirmation; cloud acceptance requires behavioral suspicion without confirmation. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G10-065)_
- **G-002/DG002-G10-066** [E-G] anchor=`G10:L230`: Dashboard-smoker verifies deltas, signals, and the honest badge. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G10-066)_
- **G-002/DG002-G10-067** [E-G] anchor=`G10:L231-L232`: Composition acceptance proves actual reuse of D-7, G-2, and F-4 identifiers rather than restatement. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G10-067)_
- **G-002/DG002-G10-068** [E-G] anchor=`G10:L234-L235`: The FP guard records rates on three real orchestrate runs before action enablement. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G10-068)_
- **G-002/DG002-G10-069** [E-G] anchor=`G10:L236-L238`: Event round trip/action lock, named schema bump, workspace cargo check, auditor passes, and zero new dependency are completion gates. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G10-069)_
- **G-002/DG002-G10-070** [E-P] anchor=`G10:L256-L260`: A drift delta is baseline-relative behavioral change, never an absolute model-capability rank. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G10-070)_
- **G-002/DG002-G10-071** [E-G] anchor=`G10:L262-L267`: Verdict contracts cover unchanged/within-band, local changed+regressed, cloud regressed, and F-4 malformed-spike cases. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G10-071)_
- **G-002/DG002-G10-072** [E-G] anchor=`G10:L268-L272`: FP contracts require no event within the band and exact seeded recomputation; a real-hook smoke test must emit on a swapped fixture identity. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G10-072)_
- **G-002/DG002-G10-073** [E-R] anchor=`G10:L318-L322`: The non-determinism band must be calibrated to avoid noise alarms; detection-only ships before any auto-pin. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G10-073)_
- **G-002/DG002-G10-074** [E-R] anchor=`G10:L323-L325`: Cloud scheduling needs a small affordable canary set; exact size trades cost against sensitivity. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G10-074)_
- **G-002/DG002-G10-075** [E-R] anchor=`G10:L328-L330`: If G-2 has not landed, identity pins may be read directly from determinism after path confirmation, though co-shipping after G-2 is preferred. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G10-075)_
- **G-002/DG002-G10-076** [E-D] anchor=`G10:L331-L332`: G-10 is not a model-selection/routing engine and not an automatic remediation loop. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G10-076)_
- **G-002/DG002-G10-077** [E-D] anchor=`G10:L334-L339`: A future bounded-FP auto-pin could become the first production emitter for wire-dark `ModelSwap`/`TierModelSwap`. _(ledger-anchor: DEEP-G-002.discovery.md; native-id: DG002-G10-077)_

### Summary invariants / claim boundaries

- **SUMMARY/INV-G-01** [E] anchor=`GSI:L11-L12, L214-L216`: Security and trust evidence is composed from F-gate verdicts; duplicate F implementations are forbidden.
- **SUMMARY/INV-G-02** [E] anchor=`GSI:L61-L64, L82-L84, L163-L165`: Missing/unsafe runtime evidence fails closed where specified: blast radius, MCP re-pin, and redaction.
- **SUMMARY/INV-G-03** [E] anchor=`GSI:L50-L50, L164-L166`: Append-only SSB rows are not updated or individually deleted; redaction happens before write and TTL is file-granular.
- **SUMMARY/INV-G-04** [E] anchor=`GSI:L217-L218`: Every emitted number is journal-recomputable and seeded.
- **SUMMARY/INV-G-05** [E] anchor=`GSI:L12-L14, L221-L222`: Default-on rollout requires empirical false-positive/over-block bounds from at least three real runs.
- **SUMMARY/INV-G-06** [E] anchor=`GSI:L25-L28`: Dependency-screened taint join is mandatory; global join is forbidden.
- **SUMMARY/INV-G-07** [E] anchor=`GSI:L46-L48`: Cloud RRA claims stop at action-stream reproduction where tokens/fingerprints are opaque.
- **SUMMARY/INV-G-08** [E] anchor=`GSI:L61-L75`: G-3 blocks outside-worktree effects before verb/connect and preserves both pre/post-expansion forensic forms.
- **SUMMARY/INV-G-09** [E] anchor=`GSI:L82-L92`: MCP schema mismatch blocks until explicit human re-approval.
- **SUMMARY/INV-G-10** [E] anchor=`GSI:L96-L100`: Reviewer findings require deterministic evidence or a failing test; model-only opinion is silent.
- **SUMMARY/INV-G-11** [E] anchor=`GSI:L113-L125`: SAFEDELTA reports marginal safety under ablation, never a SWE-bench rank.
- **SUMMARY/INV-G-12** [E] anchor=`GSI:L134-L143`: Quarantined untrusted text receives no tools and cannot reach the tool caller.
- **SUMMARY/INV-G-13** [E] anchor=`GSI:L150-L159`: Attestation verifies offline without rerunning ELAI and is not marketed as certification.
- **SUMMARY/INV-G-14** [E] anchor=`GSI:L163-L175`: Raw detected secrets must never reach either SSB DB or hook log.
- **SUMMARY/INV-G-15** [E] anchor=`GSI:L187-L207`: G-10 surfaces drift and routes review; it does not auto-swap by default.
- **SUMMARY/INV-G-16** [E] anchor=`GSI:L200-L203`: Local drift may be fingerprint-confirmed; opaque-cloud drift is only behavioral-suspected.

## Cross-file interaction records (IX-*)

### G-001/IX

- **G-001/DG001-XINT-001** [E] [interaction] anchor=`G0:L45-L93`: Shared governance: every G item inherits W-1..W-10 and GD-1..GD-5 → real entrypoint/effect/SSB/UI evidence → FP bound → auditor/wiring/dashboard/memory gates → only then completion.

### SUMMARY/WF

- **SUMMARY/WF-G-01** [E] [interaction] name=`G-1 taint flow`: Web/MCP/file/sandbox ingress constructs `Provenance` -> `Provenanced<String>` travels through agent-pool dispatch -> E-37 can seed `Untrusted` -> `allow_tool_with_origin` evaluates the live taint before git/Write/Bash -> the destructive action is blocked or confirmation-routed -> `taint_flow` records the origin -> the dashboard drawer exposes it. (GSI:L20-L36)
- **SUMMARY/WF-G-02** [E] [interaction] name=`G-2 artifact/replay`: production run collects the append-only SSB stream, determinism/model/hardware pins, and consumed F-verdict hashes -> msgpack+zstd bundle receives a BLAKE3 root -> `.rra` and `run_artifact_emitted` are produced -> viewer/`rra show` inspect it -> `replay --verify` reproduces the action stream and rechecks verdict hashes -> PASS or first-event DIVERGENCE is reported. (GSI:L38-L57)
- **SUMMARY/WF-G-03** [E] [interaction] name=`G-3 filesystem gate`: shell input is captured -> tilde/glob/symlink expansion is canonicalized -> resolved target is checked against worktree -> operation fails closed before the verb if outside -> both command forms land in `blast_radius_blocked`. (GSI:L59-L75)
- **SUMMARY/WF-G-04** [E] [interaction] name=`G-3 egress gate`: hostname is checked for null/control bytes -> allowed host is resolved -> its IP is pinned host-side -> connect is blocked before dispatch on violation -> `blast_radius_blocked` records the forensic command context. (GSI:L61-L75)
- **SUMMARY/WF-G-05** [E] [interaction] name=`G-4 MCP re-pin`: first connection is TOFU-approved -> `notifications/tools/list_changed` arrives -> tool-list hash is recomputed -> mismatch becomes `PinStatus::Violation` -> tool is blocked fail-closed -> description diff is journaled/displayed -> only explicit operator re-approval unblocks it. (GSI:L79-L92)
- **SUMMARY/WF-G-06** [E] [interaction] name=`G-5 review`: dashboard/CLI reads RRA plus SSB proof tokens -> diff is divided into vouched/un-vouched hunk bands -> only deterministic verdicts/failing tests surface -> DSR is computed and journaled -> reviewer skims vouched material and focuses on un-vouched/low-confidence material -> controlled study measures time-to-first-approve with escaped defects as guardrail. (GSI:L94-L109, L143)
- **SUMMARY/WF-G-07** [E] [interaction] name=`G-6 SAFEDELTA`: under a fixed seed, git-stripped held-out tasks execute across gate-on/gate-off and secondary competitor arms -> dynamic exploit checks and cheat detector guard evaluation -> F-4 binds the result -> `safety_delta_measured` plus signed replayable manifest are emitted -> headline, bootstrap CI, and CWE breakdown are derived. (GSI:L111-L127)
- **SUMMARY/WF-G-08** [E] [interaction] name=`G-7 confidence`: three confidence signals are fused -> existing conformal `decide()` consumes the scalar -> low confidence produces a journaled abstention/held change -> G-5 reads that band first. (GSI:L129-L143)
- **SUMMARY/WF-G-09** [E] [interaction] name=`G-7 trifecta`: G-1 detects trusted-secret + untrusted + egress in one context -> only the untrusted span enters a tool-less quarantined sub-LLM -> it never reaches the tool caller -> `trifecta_quarantined` proves the isolation. (GSI:L134-L143)
- **SUMMARY/WF-G-10** [E] [interaction] name=`G-8 attestation`: `elai run` builds the in-toto predicate from run identity/evidence -> signs locally by default -> writes digest-only commit trailer, sidecar, and SSB event -> offline verifier resolves a commit and prints verified/not-verified without rerunning ELAI. (GSI:L145-L159)
- **SUMMARY/WF-G-11** [E] [interaction] name=`G-9 persistence`: raw event/hook payload reaches the write boundary -> local fail-closed detector replaces secrets/PII with stable keyed tokens -> only redacted rows/logs persist -> retention removes the entire expired session DB. (GSI:L161-L175)
- **SUMMARY/WF-G-12** [E] [interaction] name=`G-10 drift`: establish model-A identity-keyed baseline -> identity change or schedule triggers canary -> model-B results are compared per family to last-good -> regression emits detailed `model_drift_suspected`, routes templates to review, and surfaces a lane-honest badge -> clean A result within the non-determinism band produces `NoDrift` and no event. (GSI:L183-L212)

### G-001 source-local A/C (also listed under per-unit above; re-indexed for the register)

Count: **42** (A=15, C=27).

- **G-001/DG001-G1-C001** [C] unit=`G-1` anchor=`G1:L63-L70`: Dated correction: `agent_pool.rs` is a sandbox subprocess launcher with no ingest/sink; real ingest is `web_research_orchestrator.rs:434`, `mcp_tool_bridge.rs:298`, `codeact_sandbox.rs:251`, and `production_agent_rpc.rs:1503`, while `production_agent_rpc.rs::translate_tool_request -> enforce_taint_sink_gate` is the live fan/sink.

- **G-001/DG001-G1-C003** [C] unit=`G-1` anchor=`G1:L73-L82, L96-L102, L148-L170, L240-L247, L270-L271, L296-L297`: The mandatory-read list, Task 2 wording, test-risk text, and stale file list still describe `agent_pool.rs` as the ingest fan, while the wiring contract/done-gate explicitly repoint to four other modules and `production_agent_rpc`.
- **G-001/DG001-G1-A002** [A] unit=`G-1` anchor=`G1:L290-L295`: The exact dependency-relevant-set algorithm remains the hard risk: too narrow misses attacks, too broad destroys utility; the decision doc must fix the chosen screen.

- **G-001/DG001-G3-C001** [C] unit=`G-3` anchor=`G3:L73-L75, L146-L150, L174-L181, L271-L284`: Contrary to the summary pass, this original repeatedly says Landlock is absent and explicitly bans any claim it is used; the implemented floor is OS-agnostic `std::fs` plus host check, with Linux network backstop only from existing veth/iptables.

### G-002 ambiguities

- **G-002/DG002-A-001** [A] anchor=`G2:L74-L82, L197`: G-2 does not define canonical msgpack ordering, schema migration/compatibility, stable float/string encoding, or event ordering needed for byte-identical roots.
- **G-002/DG002-A-002** [A] anchor=`G2:L58-L59, L157-L158, L266-L267`: The compression codec is intentionally unresolved; the source says in-tree codec and forbids assuming zstd without confirmation.
- **G-002/DG002-A-003** [A] anchor=`G2:L81-L82, L268-L269`: Merkle leaf format, tree shape, domain separation, duplicate-leaf handling, and inclusion-proof format are absent.
- **G-002/DG002-A-004** [A] anchor=`G2:L38-L44, L77-L79`: Criteria for `ReproducibleTokens` versus `ReproducibleActionStream` on a local run are not operationally defined.
- **G-002/DG002-A-005** [A] anchor=`G2:L84-L90`: “Fresh recompute where inputs are present” does not define input discovery, unavailable-field status, or whether missing evidence fails verification.
