### G-002 conflicts

- **G-002/DG002-C-001** [C] anchor=`G2:L3 versus G8:L21-L24, L84-L85`: G-2’s own header says `PLANNED`, while G-8’s corrected state says the G-2 RRA landed “this run.”
- **G-002/DG002-C-002** [C] anchor=`G6:L24, L65, L81; G9:L22-L23, L64, L79 versus G8:L21-L24, L84-L85`: G-6 and G-9 call `rra.rs` absent/forward-dependent, while G-8 calls it shipped.
- **G-002/DG002-C-003** [C] anchor=`G10:L28-L29 versus L73-L74`: G-10’s prerequisite says G-2 is shipped or co-shipped, while its existing-state block says `RunArtifact` is planned.
- **G-002/DG002-C-004** [C] anchor=`G2:L92-L95, L141-L150, L189-L191 versus L232-L245`: G-2’s reconciled contract says RRA emission occurs at `run_async` exit and explicitly not in `build_production_hook_dispatcher`; older Task 3 and files-touched still assign `runtime_setup.rs`.
- **G-002/DG002-C-005** [C] anchor=`G10:L95-L96, L122-L127, L180-L185 versus G2:L141-L150`: G-10 names `build_production_hook_dispatcher` as the run-end drift hook, while G-2’s correction says that dispatcher is a tool-use dispatcher, not the artifact run-exit hook.
- **G-002/DG002-C-006** [C] anchor=`G8:L3 versus L21-L24, L84-L85, L155-L170, L286-L288`: G-8’s header remains `PLANNED`, while its corrected contract calls the digest trailer, verify CLI, manifest dependency, and run-exit emit path shipped/live.
- **G-002/DG002-C-007** [C] anchor=`G8:L21-L24, L84-L85, L157, L179 versus L94-L95, L235-L238`: G-8’s corrected blocks say `rra.rs` shipped, while older mandatory reads and W-7 prose still say it is a forward dependency/absent.
- **G-002/DG002-C-008** [C] anchor=`G8:L108-L113 versus L156-L168`: G-8 Task 2 calls the trailer `provenance-digest`, whereas corrected consumed/reachable wiring names `ELAI-Provenance-Digest`.
- **G-002/DG002-C-009** [C] anchor=`G8:L99-L106, L145-L149 versus L194`: G-8 tasks produce `InTotoPredicate`, but the deterministic check requires `InTotoStatement`.
- **G-002/DG002-C-010** [C] anchor=`G2:L74-L80 versus G8:L156-L160`: G-2 defines `RunArtifact.evidence_manifest` and `GateVerdictHash.ssb_event_id`; G-8’s corrected consumer refers to `RunArtifact.manifest` and `GateVerdictHash.ssb_event_seq`.
- **G-002/DG002-C-011** [C] anchor=`G8:L99-L105 versus L155-L160, L179`: G-8 Task 1 says `build_attestation` reads SSB payloads directly, while corrected wiring says it reads the G-2 `RunArtifact` manifest and must not fork extraction.
- **G-002/DG002-C-012** [C] anchor=`G9:L7-L10, L37-L38 versus L113-L117, L292-L295`: G-9 initially says individual rows cannot be deleted, yet its honest shared-DB caveat permits a session-scoped row purge at a maintenance boundary.
- **G-002/DG002-C-013** [C] anchor=`SUM:L307-L310 versus G8:L5-L8, L178, L202`: The permitted summary requires both BLAKE3 and SHA-256 subject digests for G-8, while the assigned original specifies BLAKE3-only and explicitly bans SHA2.
- **G-002/DG002-C-014** [C] anchor=`SUM:L349-L350, L366 versus G9:L60-L63, L111-L117, L170`: The permitted summary treats G-9 TTL as whole-file deletion of an expired per-session database; the original says there is one shared database and that exact framing is false.
- **G-002/DG002-C-015** [C] anchor=`SUM:L97-L100 versus G2:L58-L59, L81-L82, L157-L158, L266-L267`: The permitted summary fixes zstd compression for G-2; the original deliberately leaves codec choice at “already-vendored” and requires confirmation before zstd.
- **G-002/DG002-C-016** [C] anchor=`SUM:L121-L124 versus G2:L141-L150, L189-L191`: The permitted summary uses `build_production_hook_dispatcher` as G-2’s production integration; the corrected original explicitly supersedes that anchor with `run_async` exit.
- **G-002/DG002-C-017** [C] anchor=`G2:L272-L276 versus G9:L22-L25, L158`: G-2 describes G-9 as its export precondition while G-9 also calls G-2 a forward dependency, yielding a build-order cycle unless “build” and “safe external release” are separated.
- **G-002/DG002-C-018** [C] anchor=`G8:L48-L53, L68-L72 versus L155-L170`: G-8’s older state says the commit path has no positive trailer writer; corrected blocks assert a shipped `PROVENANCE_TRAILER_KEY` and live binding.

### SUMMARY ambiguity sets (initiative-level)

- **SUMMARY/SUM-AMB-G-0** [A] initiative=`G-0`: Exact W-1..W-10 and GD-1..GD-5 checker mechanics, schemas, authoring rules, evidence format, and failure codes.
- **SUMMARY/SUM-AMB-G-1** [A] initiative=`G-1`: Exact provenance enum/lattice, Trusted-secret representation, dependency-screened join algorithm, dispatch ownership, which flows block versus confirm, `taint_flow` schema, and definition/sample method for false positives.
- **SUMMARY/SUM-AMB-G-2** [A] initiative=`G-2`: RRA envelope/versioning, zstd settings, ordering/canonical serialization, root coverage, manifest schema, event replay semantics, tool-body availability, system-fingerprint abstraction, CLI exit codes, divergence format, viewer behavior, and optional journal-resume boundary.
- **SUMMARY/SUM-AMB-G-3** [A] initiative=`G-3`: Shell-expansion mechanism and quoting semantics, race/TOCTOU protection, worktree-root derivation, symlink/canonicalization failure handling, DNS rebinding lifetime, IP/port/protocol policy, credential-broker role, event schema, and non-Linux roadmap.
- **SUMMARY/SUM-AMB-G-4** [A] initiative=`G-4`: Tool-list canonicalization, before/after diff format, approval storage/expiry, notification race handling, schema-vs-description hash coverage, multi-server identity, event fields, and UI action semantics.
- **SUMMARY/SUM-AMB-G-5** [A] initiative=`G-5`: Exact hunk-vouching rule, line-count treatment, renamed/binary/generated files, DSR event fold, proof-token rendering, navigation/actions, evidence-staleness behavior, study sample size/randomization, and escaped-defect definition.
- **SUMMARY/SUM-AMB-G-6** [A] initiative=`G-6`: Matrix orchestration, frozen-model identity, exact task split/deduplication, competitor configuration fairness, exploit sandboxing, cheat-detector behavior, manifest signature, bootstrap unit, power threshold, suite pooling, and external-claim gate.
- **SUMMARY/SUM-AMB-G-7** [A] initiative=`G-7`: Signal normalization/weights, Platt training/evaluation data, tau update lifecycle, missing-signal behavior, event fields, exact lethal-trifecta predicate, span extraction, quarantined-model selection, return-data sanitization, and review hold/release action.
- **SUMMARY/SUM-AMB-G-8** [A] initiative=`G-8`: Predicate type URI/schema/version, digest canonicalization, key generation/storage/rotation, Merkle proof format, cosign/Rekor behavior, sidecar naming/lookup, trailer format, offline trust roots, CLI exit codes, SSB action type, and Article-50 wording placement.
- **SUMMARY/SUM-AMB-G-9** [A] initiative=`G-9`: Secret/PII detector patterns, key source/rotation for stable tokens, collision/domain separation, fail-closed failure mode, redaction of nested/binary data, debug access, session TTL configuration, purge scheduling/locking, crash consistency, and false-positive study.
- **SUMMARY/SUM-AMB-G-10** [A] initiative=`G-10`: Identity canonicalization, baseline persistence/versioning, canary-set composition, schedule configuration, regression threshold/aggregation, last-good promotion, Pattern-2 schema, rerun cost controls, template review/revert workflow, lane badge derivation, and handling of missing/changed fingerprints.

## Inference records (I-*)

### G-001/INF

- **G-001/DG001-INF-001** [I] basis=`High-confidence textual inference from “shipped” identifiers/baselines, but no source changes overall status to DONE; do not promote it.`: The dated correction blocks look like post-implementation backfills, so G-1/G-4/G-5/G-7 may be partially or fully landed despite `PLANNED` headers.
- **G-001/DG001-INF-002** [I] basis=`Implied by `TaintedContext` and process-global accessor; lifecycle is not specified.`: G-1’s process-global task/turn side-table needs concurrency isolation, cleanup, and task-ID collision policy.
- **G-001/DG001-INF-003** [I] basis=`Canonicalize-then-execute creates a potential race, but the source never defines descriptor-based execution or retry semantics.`: G-3 needs TOCTOU-resistant binding between canonical validation and the actual filesystem/connect operation.
- **G-001/DG001-INF-004** [I] basis=`The declared event payload lacks descriptions; no hidden storage may be assumed.`: G-4 either needs to persist `ToolDescDiff` elsewhere or expand its SSB payload for the dashboard’s exact before/after view.
- **G-001/DG001-INF-005** [I] basis=`The declared payload has aggregate counts, while per-hunk line provenance remains underspecified.`: G-5’s aggregate result row can reproduce the DSR division but cannot alone re-derive which lines/hunks were vouched.
- **G-001/DG001-INF-006** [I] basis=`Cross-source schedule supports the ordering; implementation availability is not independently verified.`: G-7’s newly explicit G-9 secret-detector bridge is sequence-compatible because G-9 is Wave 2 and G-7 Wave 3, but it is still a missing declared prerequisite.

### G-002/I

- **G-002/DG002-I-001** [I] basis=`G-2 is the keystone while both G-2 and G-9 call G-9 an export precondition; exact release sequencing is not specified.`: Delivery can implement G-2’s local artifact before G-9, but any external RRA release should wait for G-9.
- **G-002/DG002-I-002** [I] basis=`G6:L126-L127; G2:L74-L90; G8:L304-L308. Signing composition is not directly wired in G-6.`: The intended signed SAFEDELTA manifest likely means a G-2 RRA plus G-8 attestation, because G-2 itself hashes but does not define a signature.
- **G-002/DG002-I-003** [I] basis=`Their schemas share seed/model/fingerprint/hash values, but no cross-artifact canonicalization contract is stated.`: G-2 roots, G-8 predicates, and G-10 baselines need a shared canonical identity/serialization convention to compare across machines.
- **G-002/DG002-I-004** [I] basis=`The sources say redaction is pre-persistence and export-safe, but run-exit ordering is not explicitly diagrammed.`: G-9 should run before G-2/G-8 serialization at run exit, otherwise already-collected raw values could enter exported material despite later sink redaction.
- **G-002/DG002-I-005** [I] basis=`Two trigger classes and one-row language are explicit; locking/deduplication is absent.`: Concurrent scheduled and run-end G-10 checks need coalescing/idempotency to avoid duplicate drift rows and canary cost.
- **G-002/DG002-I-006** [I] basis=`Core redaction is default-on and advisory wire-dark; the panel promises counts but the counter store is not fixed.`: The optional G-9 advisory cannot be the sole source for dashboard counts because it may be disabled while redaction remains on.
- **G-002/DG002-I-007** [I] basis=``identity_key` includes model/provider/lane, while the signal name is narrower; source does not resolve mapping.`: A G-10 `FingerprintChanged` signal should probably be reserved for actual fingerprint change, not every `identity_key` change.
- **G-002/DG002-I-008** [I] basis=`The source uses “signed” and `sign_local_merkle` but defines no secret/private key or external trust root.`: A local Merkle tree proves integrity only relative to a trusted root; calling it “signed” likely requires an unstated trust/key binding.

### SUMMARY/I-G

- **SUMMARY/I-G-01** [I] caution=`High: an implementation ordering G-2 -> those consumers is directly implied, but exact delivery phases are not specified beyond waves.`: G-2 is called the keystone and explicitly anchors G-5/G-6/G-8/G-9.

- **SUMMARY/I-G-04** [I] caution=`High, but the summary does not specify sidecar lookup, trailer parsing, or key storage details.`: G-8 can verify evidence off-machine because G-2 supplies a portable manifest and SSB chain.
- **SUMMARY/I-G-05** [I] caution=`High: explicitly excluded from the done-gate; no automatic rollback algorithm may be assumed.`: G-10's future auto-pin would be a separate human-gated remediation workflow after detection.
